Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the structure object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 13. The moduli space has dimension at most 49 with 3 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the structure space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 13 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 49. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
